## EV-052 — DRAFT set member equation (K-14)

- revision: 1
- standing: DRAFT — PROPOSED reviewed K-14 membership equation with canonical primitive truth results.
- kind: requirement
- subject: an evaluator build computing member(s,v) for valid set s and any admitted evaluator query value v
- from: SOURCE R6_RULES_BASE:637–648 (EV-052, pinned R5); inherited R5 citation scope follows: SOURCE OLD_RULES:644–655 (EV-052); earlier source/review/decision provenance and standing as recorded in this pinned R4 base, not reopened original files. Current updates: SOURCE CARD:40–47; ARCH:21–30,44–80,118–121; six authenticated owner records and OWNER_LINE_VERIFICATION.json.
- text: member(s,v) returns canonical truth.yes exactly when v belongs to M(s), and canonical truth.no otherwise. Its query domain is every admitted evaluator value, including direct and nested sequence identities. This observes fixed membership without changing the set or consulting a sequence's contents/head for comparison. A user-constructed truth case with another payload is not substituted for the canonical primitive result.
- case: PROPOSED Apply F in both Sigma0 and Sigma1. For each j=0–4, member(Sj,wj)=Case(truth/2,1,Row()), member(Sj,b)=Case(truth/2,0,Row()), member(Uj,wj)=truth.yes, member(Uj,b)=truth.yes and member(E,wj)=truth.no. For j=0, the absent b returns no even while a and b have equal contents. insert(Sj,wj)=Sj and remove(Sj,b)=Sj exactly, with members(Sj)=Row(wj) unchanged in both states. The comparisons do not alter either supplied sequence's head.
- excludes: PROPOSED Returning yes solely from equal sequence contents, returning no after a member's contents/head change, returning a natural or a noncanonical truth payload, changing a head through identity comparison, or mutating/reordering the set through membership testing.
- gaps: Preserve this DRAFT extensional equation and shared canonical set invariant for all incoming, literal and reachable-starting-state sets. The every-admitted-value all-depth member/query domain is decided; wrong-kind non-set operands return none under EV-084. Malformed admission, full traversal, canonical bytes, exact interfaces and complete result/temporary/work/debit costs remain E-4/E-14/E-16/E-18/E-27/E-28. ## EV-053 — DRAFT set size equation (K-14)

- revision: 1
- standing: DRAFT — PROPOSED reviewed K-14 cardinality equation.

- text: size(s) returns the natural cardinality of M(s), counting each distinct immediate member once. It leaves the canonical immutable set unchanged. Contained rows, cases, nested sets and sequence identities are values, not extra members to count by traversing their internals or sequence state.
- case: PROPOSED Apply F in both Sigma0 and Sigma1. size(E)=0, size(Sj)=1 and size(Uj)=2 for each j=0–4. Thus size(S3)=1 although its sole member is a nonempty set, and size(S4)=1 despite the depth-four sequence path. Duplicate insert(Sj,wj)=Sj and absent remove(Sj,b)=Sj exactly; their size is still 1 and members row is still Row(wj). No size or canonical set identity/order changes when a's contents/head change.
- excludes: PROPOSED Counting nested members/row fields/sequence positions as additional members of the outer set; counting duplicate insertion twice; shrinking on an absent query; changing the original set; or making size depend on the mutable state reachable through a member.

## EV-054 — DRAFT set members equation (K-14)

- revision: 1
- standing: DRAFT — PROPOSED reviewed K-14 ordered-extraction equation.
- kind: requirement
- subject: an evaluator build computing members(s) for a valid admitted finite set s under the conditional K-3 order
- from: SOURCE R6_RULES_BASE:661–672 (EV-054, pinned R5); inherited R5 citation scope follows: SOURCE OLD_RULES:668–679 (EV-054); earlier source/review/decision provenance and standing as recorded in this pinned R4 base, not reopened original files. Current updates: SOURCE CARD:40–47; ARCH:21–30,44–80,118–121; six authenticated owner records and OWNER_LINE_VERIFICATION.json.
- text: members(s) returns the immutable row containing every member of M(s) exactly once in increasing K-3 order, and no other field, without changing s. A sequence occurs as its stable identity, including inside another immutable value; extraction neither replaces it by its contents nor uses its contents/head to order it. Equal canonical sets therefore have the same ordered member row, independent of construction history and later sequence-state changes.
- case: PROPOSED Apply F in both Sigma0 and Sigma1. members(E)=Row(), members(Sj)=Row(wj), and members(Uj)=Row(wj,b) for all j=0–4. In particular members(U0)=Row(a,b) even when their current contents coincide. insert(Sj,wj)=Sj and remove(Sj,b)=Sj exactly. The row obtained from each Sj in Sigma0 still contains the same wj in Sigma1, and a new members(Sj) has the same row value/order; no snapshot of a's old contents is substituted. Extracting Uj after the opposite insertion order gives the same canonical set Uj and exactly Row(wj,b).
- excludes: PROPOSED Omitting or repeating a member; returning Row(b,wj); sorting by insertion history, current sequence contents or head; returning recursively captured contents in place of identities; changing an old extracted row; or mutating the set during extraction.
- gaps: Preserve this DRAFT extensional equation and shared canonical set invariant for all incoming, literal and reachable-starting-state sets. The every-admitted-value all-depth member/query domain is decided; wrong-kind non-set operands return none under EV-084. Malformed admission, full traversal, canonical bytes, exact interfaces and complete result/temporary/work/debit costs remain E-4/E-14/E-16/E-18/E-27/E-28. No physical-allocation or zero-temporary claim follows. R6: EV-086–113 now draft the listed K-13 operation table and E/L/S after bounded G0172 review; this does not complete or adopt the whole meter. R10 drafts only the EV-122–125 structural/no-turn/entry/admission-boundary/unit proposal. ## EV-060 — Resolved COUNT: distance from last access

- revision: 2
- standing: working policy — separately verified owner decision; the wording is bounded to that answer, without complete deferred operation/state/meter adoption.
- kind: requirement
- subject: an evaluator build under the narrowly resolved policy
- from: SOURCE R6_RULES_BASE:733–744 (EV-060, pinned R5); inherited R5 citation scope follows: SOURCE OLD_RULES:740–751 (EV-060); earlier source/review/decision provenance and standing as recorded in this pinned R4 base, not reopened original files. - text: Positional movement is counted from the last access/place of that sequence rather than from the start on every access. The current place is therefore relevant state. This answer supplies no complete tariff, base unit, head initialization, empty-sequence behavior, access/stop/contents transition or restore debit.
- case: Supply an already established last-access place 2 in a length-3 sequence. A permitted indexed write at 2 has movement distance 0, rather than distance 2 from the start. This distinguishes the counting source without assigning total fuel; the strict interface supplies no positional sequence-read primitive.
- excludes: Reinitializing the counting source to the start for every access, or asserting distance+1/full fuel/head initialization from this answer alone.
- gaps: Current policy: strict copy-then-row reading, fixed length, every-admitted-value all-depth set domain, start-derived logical scope, no whole-sequence write, invalid memo-mark refusal, last-access counting and zero-origin positions. ## EV-062 — Start-derived logical sequence identity and nonrewindable supply (K-1)

- revision: 2
- standing: DRAFT — PROPOSED bounded K-1 core marked READY for drafting by C_R2/C_R3; no adoption or implementation acceptance.
- kind: definition
- subject: an evaluator build on an admitted complete fresh start, or a same-run resume distinguished as below, under the unresolved common canonical-byte/hash and collision/refusal premises
- from: SOURCE R6_RULES_BASE:757–768 (EV-062, pinned R5); inherited R5 citation scope follows: SOURCE C_R2:25,42,46–50,79–81; C_R3:16,22,35,41,82–92; ARCH:25–30; independently authenticated ZERO_START /owner_line and /authorizes_only; OLD_RULES:704–714. Older Arch coordinates in C remain provenance only. R12: A5_R2:54-170,174-289,424-428; G0181:9-14,37-39; C_ID:1,9,137-149; card0470.
- text: PROPOSED A created sequence has an identity (rho,c): rho comes from that logical run's complete start under the common canonical-start/hash profile, and c is its creation count. Identical complete logical starts intentionally use the same rho; separate wall-clock invocations are not promised different names. A fresh entry begins with creation supply zero and an empty memo store. Replay of that fresh form uses its recorded complete start and repeats its creation counts. Imported identities retain their supplied pairs, and an imported pair must not be silently rebound to conflicting contents or last places. A new creation consumes the next same-run count; the allocation high-water supply never decreases or reuses a consumed count, including after going back. A same-run resume therefore retains that high-water supply and its current run scope rather than pretending to be a count-zero fresh entry. Required framing, complete graph order, domain separation and collision refusal are obligations of the common profile, not a completed recipe here. A name correspondence used for a continuation fixes identities made before the point and preserves the order of later identities; order preservation alone does not preserve the charge of a name operation. SOURCE — Start normalization uses numbered local variables, retains represented definition/choice identities and includes every declaration, including unused. Sequence identity order is fixed independently of the current run by scope length, scope bytes, creation count. [decision: owner-record-2026-11-11-oc-05-09-recs.json; OC-05 B; OC-06 Keep; OC-07 A; clarifies owner-record-2026-11-11-oc-03-05-08.json] PROPOSED — Exact codecs/hash/profile/admission remain prerequisites.
- case: PROPOSED Two fresh entries with the same complete canonical start and two corresponding completed makes produce (rho,0), then (rho,1), under sufficient fuel. These are the same logical names across the two entries. In one of those logical runs, keep a point before those two makes, complete both, and go back under the separately specified paid restoration protocol: the next creation uses (rho,2), not (rho,0). An import presentation that assigns one supplied pair two different contents or heads is refused rather than merged. This fixture does not calculate rho or its bytes.
- excludes: PROPOSED Adding an arbitrary per-occasion nonce to distinguish identical logical starts; equating distinct imported pairs merely because their current contents agree; silently merging conflicting state under one pair; resetting the same-run high-water supply on restoration; treating an order-preserving renaming as proof of equal fuel or equal out-of-fuel points.
- gaps: E-1/E-16/E-23 retain exact canonical program/value/start bytes, graph presentation, named hash/domain/collision/import validation and executable replay interfaces. The start-derived source is answered, and the core is drafted rather than deferred. Complete resumed/warmed entry records, allocation/rollback interaction with outside facts and the full continuation/projection/fuel theorem remain E-26/E-35–37. No distinct-occasion freshness, collision-freedom proof, complete meter, native result or phase authority is claimed. R6: EV-086–113 now draft the listed K-13 operation table and E/L/S after bounded G0172 review; this does not complete or adopt the whole meter. R10 drafts only the EV-122–125 structural/no-turn/entry/admission-boundary/unit proposal. Remaining natural and byte entries, positive-loop setup/turn A/R/T/counter charges, separate admission resources, complete meter integration, EV-018/021 witnesses, exact spellings and any host calibration outside semantic units remain open. R10 N10 supplies the conditional fresh completed-unary EV-024/026 witnesses; incomplete/warmed/resumed/restoration and general integration remain open. The full mixed-kind R3 comparison-event schedule is referenced but unpinned and unopened. R12 EV-132 supplies a conditional candidate start/name fixture only; H, faithful full normalization codec, import/profile/collision/replay remain open. SOURCE — Numbered locals, Keep and fixed order are selected. [decision: owner-record-2026-11-11-oc-05-09-recs.json; OC-05 B; OC-06 Keep; OC-07 A; clarifies owner-record-2026-11-11-oc-03-05-08.json] PROPOSED — current-scope equality is not automatically a proved collision.

## EV-063 — Complete initial/live graph and saved-state distinction (K-4)

- revision: 1
- standing: DRAFT — PROPOSED complete initial/live-state core and saved-state/supply distinction marked READY by C_R2/C_R3.
- kind: definition
- subject: an evaluator build admitting a complete initial sequence graph and maintaining a complete live configuration
- from: SOURCE R6_RULES_BASE:769–780 (EV-063, pinned R5); inherited R5 citation scope follows: SOURCE C_R2:23,26,43,46,54,79–85; C_R3:15–17,49,82–92; ARCH:25–30,71–74,118–121; OLD_GAPS:251–259. Empty/last-place edge authority is separately authenticated through DROP_STRICT and OWNER_LINE_VERIFICATION.json; see EV-080/081. R12: A5_R2:54-170,174-289,424-428; G0181:9-14,37-39; C_ID:1,9,137-149; card0470.
- text: PROPOSED For this fresh-entry profile, program literals may contain no sequence handle, including at immutable depth. The initial sequence graph is the transitive closure from the input through rows, case payloads, set members and sequence contents; aliases and cycles are followed by identity, with one consistent state for each supplied pair. For every reached sequence the initial graph supplies its fixed length, all current held values and its last place. Missing contents, conflicting contents or conflicting heads are refused. Initial head validity obeys the separately bound edge rules: a nonempty sequence has a head in its zero-origin range; an empty sequence uses 0 as a marker naming no position; other arriving heads are refused. During execution, the complete live configuration includes control and continuation state, live bindings and frames, handlers, loop state, activation and use-once information, memo entries, pending/result roots, and every sequence reachable through those roots and through held values, with contents and heads. Runtime allocation supply is recorded separately from the execution-state projection saved for restoration: the same-run high-water supply is never restored backward. Going back neither reinstates spent fuel nor erases the full trail. Detailed restoration of the saved execution projection must respect the separately specified activation/use-once/outside-fact boundaries; this definition does not supply that protocol.
- case: PROPOSED Supply admitted input Row(s), where s holds Row(t) and t holds Row(s), with their valid heads. The initial graph contains both sequences exactly once by identity despite the cycle. Omit t's contents, or give an alias of s a conflicting head: refuse the start. Put the same handle in a program literal with input 0: refuse it under this profile, rather than using that literal while taking an input-only state snapshot. At a later point retain a newly made u only in a live frame or memo/result root: u remains part of the live graph. Restoring an older execution point does not reset the allocation high-water supply or refill fuel.
- excludes: PROPOSED Recording only top-level handles; dropping sequence-contents links, cycles, heads, control/handler/activation/use-once state, live-frame/loop/memo/result roots; running with a missing or conflicting imported state; admitting a handle-bearing program literal under this input-only fresh-entry policy; confusing the saved execution-state projection with the nonrewindable allocation supply or an old fuel account.
- gaps: E-4/E-16/E-25 retain executable graph admission/decoder/encoding and exact complete-configuration presentation. The input/live root core and saved-state/supply split are now drafted. Checkpoint capture cost, two-phase restore integration, interruption/committed-prefix behavior, ownership/ABA/concurrency and activation/use-once/outside-fact interaction remain E-35–37; no complete restoration engine or meter is defined. The separate current edge records supply the authoritative empty/invalid-head policy, not this leaf's owner audit. R6: EV-086–113 now draft the listed K-13 operation table and E/L/S after bounded G0172 review; this does not complete or adopt the whole meter. R10 drafts only the EV-122–125 structural/no-turn/entry/admission-boundary/unit proposal. Remaining natural and byte entries, positive-loop setup/turn A/R/T/counter charges, separate admission resources, complete meter integration, EV-018/021 witnesses, exact spellings and any host calibration outside semantic units remain open. R10 N10 supplies the conditional fresh completed-unary EV-024/026 witnesses; incomplete/warmed/resumed/restoration and general integration remain open. The full mixed-kind R3 comparison-event schedule is referenced but unpinned and unopened. R12 imported-state layout supplies only represented framing. Repeated pairs still need fixed length, all contents and head agreement plus all other admission conditions; full graph/profile/origin validation remains.

## EV-064 — Fresh-only build-relative record core (K-12)

- revision: 1
- standing: DRAFT — PROPOSED fresh-only, build-relative record core marked READY by C_R2/C_R3; full evidence/replay forms remain open.
- kind: requirement
- subject: an evaluator build producing or interpreting a record of a fresh count-zero, empty-memo entry
- from: SOURCE R6_RULES_BASE:781–792 (EV-064, pinned R5); inherited R5 citation scope follows: SOURCE C_R2:25,27,29–30,44,46,83–85; C_R3:17,82–92; ARCH:25–30,120; OLD_RULES:344–450; OLD_GAPS:261–269. R12: A5_R2:54-170,174-289,424-428; G0181:9-14,37-39; C_ID:1,9,137-149; card0470.
- text: PROPOSED A record in this form names the executing build, program and input; holds the complete admitted initial graph with its initial heads and start-derived scope by value; explicitly records the count-zero/empty-memo entry policy; records the supplied fuel bound and terminal outcome; retains the make/write events in their actual order; and records final heads. A successful outcome holds its returned value, interpreted against the final sequence state reconstructed from the immutable initial graph and ordered events. An out-of-fuel outcome is recorded as that outcome rather than a fabricated returned value. The required start and event data are immutable by-value evidence, not live aliases that can change after delivery; the events must retain enough information to reconstruct the represented final contents and new sequences without consulting a later mutable heap. Final heads are necessary because contents reads can move a head without a write. Preserve the richer associated immutable observation context owed by the breadcrumb contracts, including terminal/held outcome, request/reply and answer-pick correlation, consumption value for value and activation for activation, and each outside reply's endpoint, version, state and row provenance. The projections and exact representations of that context remain obligations rather than completed schemas. This form is relative to the named build; bringing its record into a second build does not mean that build has replayed it. It is a fresh-entry form: warmed or resumed entry needs a different explicit record form. Recording an evaluator result alone does not establish a checker's separate statement-form/theory/scope soundness claim.
- case: PROPOSED A fresh run starts with input Row(s), initial s holding 0 at head 0, zero supply and empty memo. It makes t, writes a represented held value, successfully returns Row(t), and records the actual build, immutable start, bound, successful output, ordered make/write information and final heads. Later mutation of live s or t cannot rewrite the recorded start/events or the endpoint reconstructed from them. In a second fixture, a successful contents(s) moves a nonempty head but makes no write; the final-head field records that movement. A bounded stopped run records OUT_OF_FUEL and its committed event prefix, without claiming a successful output. These are semantic record fixtures under the eventual complete meter, not numeric executions.
- excludes: PROPOSED Omitting the build or complete initial graph/heads; replacing supplied fuel with remaining fuel; using mutable aliases for historical start/events; treating an ordered write trail as sufficient to recover final heads after reads; inventing a successful output for OUT_OF_FUEL; interpreting a returned nested handle against its later live contents; presenting a warmed/resumed entry as a count-zero empty-memo fresh record; equating record import with replay by the importing build; deriving checker soundness from an ordinary affirmative output.
- gaps: E-12/E-16/E-26 retain exact record bytes, outcome/result presentation, parser/authentication/replay and complete state-qualified Eval inference. The fresh field core is drafted. Resumed/warmed forms, detached output/deep-capture operations, request shapes and the three rich observation projections remain open under E-15/E-21/E-26/E-35–37. This core supplies necessary evidence structure without a complete breadcrumb/restore/fuel equivalence theorem, a full K-13 table, conformance, adoption or implementation acceptance. R6: EV-086–113 now draft the listed K-13 operation table and E/L/S after bounded G0172 review; this does not complete or adopt the whole meter. R10 drafts only the EV-122–125 structural/no-turn/entry/admission-boundary/unit proposal. Remaining natural and byte entries, positive-loop setup/turn A/R/T/counter charges, separate admission resources, complete meter integration, EV-018/021 witnesses, exact spellings and any host calibration outside semantic units remain open. R10 N10 supplies the conditional fresh completed-unary EV-024/026 witnesses; incomplete/warmed/resumed/restoration and general integration remain open. The full mixed-kind R3 comparison-event schedule is referenced but unpinned and unopened. R12 EV-133 supplies a candidate fresh-record fixture and explicit logged/omitted-read variants only. Build naming, wire/graph admission, rich observations, paid restoration and warmed/resumed/replay premises remain; EV-C1 remains OPEN.

- from: SOURCE R6_RULES_BASE:793–804 (EV-065, pinned R5); inherited R5 citation scope follows: SOURCE OLD_RULES:548–558; READ_VARIANTS:37–41; ARCH:48–53,103,120; DROP_STRICT /owner_line,/authorizes_only and independently checked DROP_STRICT_LINES:1.

## EV-066 — Strict length operational supplement

- revision: 1
- standing: DRAFT — strict-core observation under inherited fixed-length policy.
- kind: requirement
- subject: an evaluator build observing the length of an admitted sequence s
- from: SOURCE R6_RULES_BASE:805–816 (EV-066, pinned R5); inherited R5 citation scope follows: SOURCE OLD_RULES:680–690 (EV-055),579; READ_VARIANTS:41,69–74; EDGES:151–154; ARCH:53,119–120.
- text: length(s) returns s's fixed number of positions. It changes neither the held values nor the last place and adds no make/write trail entry. This observation is permitted by the conservative state-free restriction when all its other requirements hold.

## EV-067 — Strict contents operational supplement

- revision: 1
- standing: DRAFT — strict contents core, with decided empty result below.
- kind: requirement
- subject: an evaluator build taking contents of an admitted sequence s of fixed length n
- from: SOURCE R6_RULES_BASE:817–828 (EV-067, pinned R5); inherited R5 citation scope follows: SOURCE OLD_RULES:572–582; READ_VARIANTS:51–54,112–120; ARCH:48–53,104–105,120; DROP_STRICT /authorizes_only and independently checked DROP_STRICT_LINES:1.
- text: contents(s) returns an immutable row of s's immediate held values at that operation. For n greater than zero, its completed last place is n−1; for n zero it returns the empty row and leaves last place 0. It does not replace any held value or add a write entry. A contained sequence contributes its stable handle, not a recursive frozen copy. Later writes to s do not alter this row's fields; later changes to a contained sequence remain changes of the same contained handle.
- case: For s holding Row(4,7), contents returns Row(4,7) and leaves last place 1. Selecting zero-origin row field 1 gives 7. Replacing s's field later does not change that row. If the row holds an inner handle t, a later mutation of t does not replace that field's identity.
- excludes: A direct position read on s; returning a mutable sequence instead of the row; changing a returned immediate field after a later outer write; recursively freezing a contained sequence's state; permitting contents under a successful state-free memo proof.
- gaps: Listed operation invoices and atomic/dynamic charge stages are now DRAFT at EV-086–113; complete surrounding cost and host integration remain open. Reusing a snapshot is correct at a position only while its relevant held value has not changed; tracking own writes requires the actual program's bookkeeping and no intervening other-holder writes. A row of stable handles is not complete historical state evidence. ## EV-068 — Strict write operational supplement

- revision: 2
- standing: DRAFT — preserves the inherited proposed valid-write result/trail core; narrow identity-preserving mutation policy retains its separate inherited standing.
- kind: requirement

- from: SOURCE R6_RULES_BASE:829–840 (EV-068, pinned R5); inherited R5 citation scope follows: SOURCE OLD_RULES:560–570; ARCH:48–53,106,120–122; DROP_STRICT /authorizes_only and independently checked DROP_STRICT_LINES:1. All aliases still denote the same changed sequence. Record the completed change in the ordered write trail under the EV-064 record core. Movement is counted from the prior last place to i.
- case: Aliases a,b denote s holding Row(4,7,8), last place 0. A completed write of 9 at position 2 leaves both denoting s holding Row(4,7,9), last place 2, and returns the inherited proposed truth.yes. The movement distance is 2 from the prior head. Leaving last place 0 fails this case even if the held-value update is correct. A later unpayable event preserves that completed prefix. [digest: DIGEST.json; headcases]
- excludes: Returning only a replacement sequence while leaving s holding Row(4,7,8); splitting aliases; a whole-sequence write; charging motion from the start each time; returning no or yes for the outside-range case whose decided result is none.

## EV-069 — Strict same operational supplement

- revision: 1
- standing: DRAFT — inherited same-identity result law under supplied identity/canonical-node premises.
- kind: requirement
- subject: an evaluator build comparing admitted values under the EV-062 and the explicit canonical-value premises
- from: SOURCE R6_RULES_BASE:841–852 (EV-069, pinned R5); inherited R5 citation scope follows: SOURCE OLD_RULES:68–78; ARCH:53,102; READ_VARIANTS:69–74.
- text: same returns canonical truth.yes exactly when its operands are one semantic node, otherwise canonical truth.no. Sequence equality uses stable identity rather than current held values or last places. It changes neither sequence contents nor last places and adds no make/write entry. Merely carrying or comparing a sequence identity is allowed by the conservative state-free restriction when every other requirement holds.
- case: Given distinct admitted handles s and t holding equal Row(0), same(s,s) gives truth.yes and same(s,t) gives truth.no. A later valid mutation of s leaves those identity results unchanged.

## EV-085 — Selected current strict read profile

- from: SOURCE R6_RULES_BASE:1033–1044 (EV-085, pinned R5); inherited R5 citation scope follows: SOURCE DROP_STRICT /owner_line,/authorizes_only; verified DROP_STRICT_LINES:1 raw SHA256 de13da5fc7b7af5ce80f01fc3df9dbeb2a0e88776032a6abfded6c022037a8e5; ARCH:44–55; EDGES:62–80; CARD:6,43.
- text: Use the strict sequence interface: make, fixed length, contents, positional write and same. To inspect a held value, obtain the immutable contents row and read that row. The last place is charging/state metadata, never a read selector. There is no current direct positional sequence-read primitive. Direct reading is a later option E-29; EV-077 is its conditional edge only.
- case: For s holding Row(4,7), take contents(s), then select immutable row field 1 to obtain 7 under zero-origin selection. No direct position-read call on s is needed or installed.
- excludes: A current direct sequence read, an operation that selects held values through the last place, or treating the later option as already selected.
- gaps: EV-065–069 draft operation details. E-29 retains real-checker deciding evidence and log before builds are frozen. ## EV-086 — K-13 argument parts

- revision: 2
- standing: DRAFT — transcription of Arch METER R5 listed proposal, positively reviewed only within G0172 scope; no tariff adoption or whole-meter completion.
- kind: definition
- subject: a listed K-13 operation after its argument values are obtained
- from: SOURCE R6_METER:36,44–46; R6_G0172:77–103; R6_CARD: Do 1. These are current pinned sources, not reopened transitive reports. R11: A2_R1:49-61,65-84,89-119,123-149,161-179; G0182:13-17,25-55; G_A2_R0:154-160; card0469.
- text: The part of an admitted natural is its bit length, with zero counting as 1. The part of a bytes value is its length plus 1. The part of every other admitted value is 1. Each listed operation uses these parts in its A entry, subject to Rule E for decided none-producing edges. R11 conditional bytes exception: only the proposed valid bytes get/slice entries EV-126/127 charge their receiver A=1 rather than its full part; concat/hash still use full parts. The bytes part remains length+1, and outside or wrong-kind Rule E still sums the actual complete parts of every supplied operand. This is a proposed semantic tariff, not a representation, scan-work or native bound theorem.
- case: parts(0)=1, parts(7)=3, parts(Bytes(ab cd))=3, and parts(Row(4,7))=1. These are argument components, not complete operation or program invoices.
- excludes: Counting natural zero as zero bits; charging a bytes argument only by its length; recursively counting a row merely to obtain its part.
- gaps: E-3/E-27: EV-122–125 draft only this control/no-turn/entry/admission-boundary/integer-unit proposal. Remaining primitive entries, positive-loop invoices, separate admission resources, complete integration and physical calibration outside semantic units remain open. EV-021 concrete unavailable-bound witness remains NOT DONE; R10 N10 supplies the conditional fresh completed-unary EV-024/026 witnesses; incomplete/warmed/resumed/restoration and general integration remain open. E-40 exact sequence spellings remain open. Nonempty mixed-kind comparison event counts reference an unpinned R3 schedule; no complete numeric search oracle is supplied. R11 explicit conditional receiver exception is stated above; it changes no bytes part or Rule E and proves no native work bound.

## EV-087 — K-13 prospective result cells

- revision: 1
- standing: DRAFT — transcription of Arch METER R5 listed proposal, positively reviewed only within G0172 scope; no tariff adoption or whole-meter completion.

- text: R charges one for each invoiced cell, covering reservation, writing, canonical lookup and the new node's stored height. The operation pays the full listed prospective R even if canonical lookup finds that node already present. Returning an empty row, empty set or builtin order/truth/option case that exists from every run start builds no cell on a return-only path; this exception does not cancel an explicit constructor R entry.
- case: With operands already available, row() still invoices R=1; remove(empty,0) invoices R=2; a generic construction of the canonical none case invoices R=2. By contrast members(empty), an ordinary edge returning none and case dispatch returning none each have R=0.
- excludes: Making canonical-constructor attempts free on reuse; charging physical allocation measurements instead of the listed semantic cells; treating a return-only exception as a blanket constructor waiver.

## EV-088 — K-13 temporary-frame component

- revision: 1
- standing: DRAFT — transcription of Arch METER R5 listed proposal, positively reviewed only within G0172 scope; no tariff adoption or whole-meter completion.
- kind: definition
- subject: a listed nonempty set-search operation
- from: SOURCE R6_METER:38,65–68,88; R6_G0172:77–103; R6_CARD: Do 1. These are current pinned sources, not reopened transitive reports.
- text: T is one per frame held at once. For nonempty set search it is the smaller of the sought value's height and the tallest member's height, plus 1. This precomputed comparison-frame bound is reserved once for the search, rather than summed again for every comparison. Empty-set operations with no comparison have T=0.
- case: Given stated height premises h(v)=2 and maximum member height 3, the nonempty-search component is T=min(2,3)+1=3. An empty-set member query instead has T=0. This component example is not a complete nonempty-search fuel oracle.
- excludes: Using zero merely because values are interned; summing separate simultaneous-frame bounds over all comparisons; interpreting the bound as measured native storage.

## EV-090 — K-13 atomic known invoice

- revision: 1
- standing: DRAFT — transcription of Arch METER R5 listed proposal, positively reviewed only within G0172 scope; no tariff adoption or whole-meter completion.
- kind: requirement
- subject: a listed K-13 operation after prior argument evaluations
- from: SOURCE R6_METER:40,44,79–80,93; R6_G0172:77–103; R6_CARD: Do 1. These are current pinned sources, not reopened transitive reports.
- text: Arguments run left to right. The operation computes A, R, T and any W known beforehand and takes their sum atomically. If available fuel does not cover that sum, it stops as out of fuel: this operation has taken no fuel and built or changed nothing. Earlier completed argument operations and their debits remain.
- case: For an already reached out-of-range write on a length-3 sequence at position 4 with supplied value 1, A=1+3+1=5 and W=1, so the invoice is 6. Fuel 5 fails before any debit/cell/last-place/trail change by that write; fuel 8 pays 6 and leaves 2 at this operation boundary.
- excludes: Partly taking an unaffordable known invoice, committing a write/head/trail before payment, or rolling back prior argument effects.
- gaps: E-3/E-27: EV-122–125 draft only this control/no-turn/entry/admission-boundary/integer-unit proposal. ## EV-091 — K-13 dynamic comparison prefix

- revision: 1
- standing: DRAFT — transcription of Arch METER R5 listed proposal, positively reviewed only within G0172 scope; no tariff adoption or whole-meter completion.
- kind: requirement
- subject: a listed search whose known invoice has been paid
- from: SOURCE R6_METER:40,88; R6_G0172:81,103; R6_G0172:77–103; R6_CARD: Do 1. These are current pinned sources, not reopened transitive reports.
- text: After the atomic known invoice, comparison events are paid one at a time before each event. No result or state change is committed until every required comparison event completes. If a later event is unaffordable, earlier paid events and debits stay paid, but that unfinished operation commits no result/state change.
- case: Protocol-only fixture: stipulate a known invoice 4 and a separately stated comparison schedule of three one-unit events. Fuel 6 pays 4 and two events, then stops before the third with zero left and no operation commit. This checks the paid-prefix rule only; it is not a numeric nonempty-set oracle or an EV-021 unavailable-bound witness.
- excludes: Refunding a paid prefix after later exhaustion; executing an event before its debit; committing a result after only a proper prefix of required events.
- gaps: E-3/E-27: EV-122–125 draft only this control/no-turn/entry/admission-boundary/integer-unit proposal. ## EV-092 — K-13 Rule E actual-argument edge invoice

- revision: 1
- standing: DRAFT — transcription of Arch METER R5 listed proposal, positively reviewed only within G0172 scope; no tariff adoption or whole-meter completion.
- kind: requirement
- subject: a primitive with a decided ordinary none result for wrong kinds or out-of-range read/slice/write
- from: SOURCE R6_METER:44–46,60,62,69,72,75,78,80,90,93,100; R6_G0172:77–103; R6_CARD: Do 1. These are current pinned sources, not reopened transitive reports.
- text: After evaluating its arguments, classify operand kinds and range before result-size, search or distance calculations. On such a decided edge, A is the sum of parts of all arguments actually supplied, R=0, T=0 and W=1. A+1 is paid atomically or not at all. Success returns the already-existing bare none and changes no cell, last place or primitive trail entry; earlier argument effects remain.
- case: Already obtained contents(256): A=bits(256)=9, R=T=0, W=1, invoice 10. Already obtained make(Bytes(ab cd ef 01),0): A=(4+1)+1=6, invoice 7 and no new sequence/count. An outside write with sequence, position 4 and value 1 costs 1+3+1+1=6.
- excludes: Using the ordinary sequence slot A=1 for natural 256; debiting a partial A+1; taking a count on rejected make; moving last place or adding a primitive write entry on none. This rule does not turn arithmetic defaults, malformed admission or an unpayable huge-left-shift bound into ordinary none.
- gaps: E-3/E-27: EV-122–125 draft only this control/no-turn/entry/admission-boundary/integer-unit proposal. ## EV-093 — K-13 Rule L rejected loop bound

- revision: 1
- standing: DRAFT — transcription of Arch METER R5 listed proposal, positively reviewed only within G0172 scope; no tariff adoption or whole-meter completion.
- kind: requirement
- subject: for x < e1 from y = e2 do e3
- from: SOURCE R6_METER:22,48,83,93,102; R6_G0172:83,92; R6_G0172:77–103; R6_CARD: Do 1. These are current pinned sources, not reopened transitive reports.
- text: Evaluate e1 then e2 once each before checking the bound. If the obtained bound is not a natural, execute no body turn, invoice A=the bound's actual part, R=T=0, W=1, and on payment return existing none. Prior bound/initializer effects and charges remain. A natural zero bound is separate: it returns the obtained initializer, executes no body turn and has no rejected-bound Rule L invoice.
- case: After its bound Bytes(61 62 63) and initializer have both been evaluated, the rejected-bound invoice is A=(3+1)=4 plus W=1, total 5. Fuel 4 at that boundary fails without this invoice's debit; fuel 7 pays 5, leaves 2 and returns none with no body. A natural-zero bound returns the initializer with no L invoice; its broader setup/entry/variable/admission gaps are not filled with zero.
- excludes: Checking the nonnatural bound before the initializer; skipping/doubling initializer effects; executing a body turn; applying the rejected-bound invoice to natural zero or pretending the stated invoice is a whole-program cost.
- gaps: E-3/E-27: EV-122–125 draft only this control/no-turn/entry/admission-boundary/integer-unit proposal. ## EV-094 — K-13 Rule S incoming last place

- revision: 1
- standing: DRAFT — transcription of Arch METER R5 listed proposal, positively reviewed only within G0172 scope; no tariff adoption or whole-meter completion.

- from: SOURCE R6_METER:50,102; R6_G0172:83; R6_G0172:77–103; R6_CARD: Do 1. These are current pinned sources, not reopened transitive reports.
- text: An incoming empty sequence is valid as to its last place exactly when that place is 0. An incoming sequence of natural length n at least 1 must carry a natural last place p with 0<=p<n. Any other starting place is refused at admission, before execution. This admission predicate has no cost entry in the listed table.
- case: Allowed (n,p) are (0,0), (3,0) and (3,2). Refuse (0,1), (3,3), a nonnatural place, or an otherwise out-of-range place before runtime. No admission fee or complete admission traversal is asserted by these controls.
- excludes: Admitting an empty sequence with another last place; treating invalid incoming metadata as a paid runtime none edge; inventing a zero admission cost.

## EV-108 — K-13 nonempty contents invoice

- revision: 3

It changes no held value and adds no make/write trail entry. Inner sequence values remain handles, not recursive snapshots. INFERENCE — Under this retained proposed table, for n>0 the own operation invoice is 1+(n+1)+(p+n)=2n+p+2. After a completed contents, with no intervening head-changing operation, another contents on that same nonempty sequence starts at p=n-1 and has own invoice 3n+1. Both expressions exclude surrounding argument evaluation and run-entry costs. This algebraic explanation chooses no tariff and preserves the shallow inner sequence handles. [digest: DIGEST.json; contentsdoc]
- case: For supplied n=3, p=0 and held Row(4,7,9), the invoice is 1+(3+1)+0+(0+3)=8. It returns that immutable row and changes last place from 0 to 2. Leaving the head at 0 fails this case even if the returned row is correct. Arguments, variable access and run-entry costs are outside this operation invoice. [digest: DIGEST.json; headcases]
- excludes: Charging only n-1-p or only the movement; leaving a completed nonempty contents at a last place other than n−1; treating the row as a deep frozen history of contained sequences.

## EV-119 — Conditional global collection, roles and lexical scopes

- revision: 1
- standing: DRAFT — PROPOSED conditional transcription of reviewed A4 R2; exact choices and independent EV-G2 review remain.
- kind: requirement
- subject: an evaluator build under a fixed candidate source profile, without adoption
- from: SOURCE R9_A4:132–158,268–269; R9_F0264:7,11,15; R9_PLAN:101,103,106–107; R9_G0178:1,7–18; card 0466.
- text: PROPOSED — Before any reference resolves, collect active primitive arities, built-in choice counts and every program choice/definition count into one table. Global declarations are pairwise distinct and unreserved. Parameters cover the whole definition body; let binds only its continuation, not initializer; branch binds only its own body, and else nothing; both loop names bind only its body, not bound or initializer. No binder escapes. Each variable spelling is bound once per definition and is never global. Calls require primitive/definition role and exact arity; constructors and case heads require choice role. Missing/wrong-role globals and held words are refused before the run. Plain REF atoms use only visible binders, never the global table, and missing binders are refused. Forward global references are allowed without reordering declarations. Every case ordinal is below its choice count, none repeats, and else is required unless all cases are covered. Resolve once before running, to fixed references, never by runtime spelling lookup.
- case: PROPOSED — S01 def f(x) = x resolves its local binder although x is absent from the global table. S02 let x = 0; x resolves only the continuation; S05 let x = x; x is refused for the initializer use. S04/S06–S09 refuse earlier, sibling/else, bound/initializer or escaped uses. N01 resolves a forward choice; N02 resolves a forward definition; N03 resolves a forward choice inside a literal. These are name derivations, not executions.
- excludes: PROPOSED — Global-table lookup of local variables; definition-wide binder visibility; shadowing/capture under this candidate; resolving only already-seen globals; silently reordering totality order; wrong-role/arity calls becoming ordinary none; runtime display-name rediscovery.
- gaps: E-4 complete presentation/admission and grammar proof; E-6 vocabulary; E-11 syntax-total implementation; E-13 memo policy/resources; E-16 encoding; E-18 interfaces; E-22/O1 general positive equation beyond selected bound two and positive-loop charges; E-24 executable visiting/resolution; E-33 gate integration; E-40 all exact word choices. All inherited meter, witness and reconsideration obligations remain. SOURCE — The former O1 owner wait is answered only at bound two (0 then1). [decision: owner-record-2026-11-11-loop-o1-a-and-arch-fixes.json; E-22/O1 A, bound two only] PROPOSED — General positive equations and positive-loop setup/turn/counter charges remain open; spelling/profile and other existing technical holds are unchanged.

## EV-121 — Conditional conservative classifier and whole-action gate

- revision: 2
- standing: DRAFT — PROPOSED conditional transcription of reviewed A4 R2; exact choices and independent EV-G2 review remain.
- kind: requirement
- subject: an evaluator build under a fixed candidate source profile, without adoption
- from: SOURCE R9_A4:213–251,265–267,271,335; R9_F0264:8–9,15–17,23–25; R9_PLAN:101,103,106–107; R9_G0178:1,7–18; card 0466. R14 sources: owner-record-2026-11-11-web-oq1-oq3-a.json#OQ1, DIGEST.json#F036.
- text: PROPOSED — For a resolved first-order definition f, its closure contains f and every definition called anywhere in any closure body, including all arguments, dead branches and recursive components. It passes exactly when no body names the fixed make, contents or write roles; unresolved callees have already been refused as text and cannot pass. Every make is forbidden even empty or unused. Immutable field/byte access has no sequence fallback; fixed length, same and handle carriage may pass without mutable-state observation. A paid immutable invocation outside its required operand domain returns none without primitive state changes, but valid reads can also return stored none. Sequence identities may occupy legal set member/query positions; a required set operand must still be a set. Prior argument effects stand, and an immutable outer call excuses none of its arguments. A failing memo mark refuses before the run. Every definition invoked by admission or result checking, including literal, constructor-field and membership checks and all callees, must pass marked or not. An unmarked stateful definition may be offered in an ordinary separately fuelled run under its other conditions; it has no admission exemption. This is exact for the syntactic forbidden-role predicate and conservative for semantic state independence; it is distinct from syntax-totality and provides no positive-loop equation. SOURCE — Computing a term once and reusing its result is permitted only when the term makes nothing new, has no effect, has no open choice, consumes nothing use-once, and has a result that may be copied. State-freedom alone is insufficient. [decision: owner-record-2026-11-11-web-oq1-oq3-a.json; OQ1 A with Arch condition] Gate PASS is only the stated effect predicate, not evidence of these additional reuse premises and not a new memo-mark refusal policy. PROPOSED — It also does not establish the whole checked-action row or termination: each invoked check and its rejection/exhaustion possibilities remain E-21. [digest: DIGEST.json; F036]
- case: PROPOSED — N9 C1–C13 are the thirteen explicit gate derivations: pure field/length/carriage/comparison and pure recursive closures pass; direct/helper/dead-branch contents, allocation and write fail. C12 has effect FAIL with no invalid mark to reject in the ordinary context, but fails admission/result eligibility. X2/X4 show that a safe outer operation or deep helper cannot hide forbidden work; X3 is a pure recursive component that passes only this effect predicate. No case claims successful evaluator execution or a complete fuel budget.
- excludes: PROPOSED — Path-pruning dead effects; omitting recursive/helper closure; pardoning argument effects; equating none with invalid operands in both directions; accepting a sequence as a required set receiver; applying the gate only to marked admission helpers; replacing syntactic conservatism by semantic completeness; treating gate PASS as totality, fuel success or adoption.
- gaps: E-4 complete presentation/admission and grammar proof; E-6 vocabulary; E-11 syntax-total implementation; E-13 memo policy/resources; E-16 encoding; E-18 interfaces; E-22/O1 general positive equation beyond selected bound two and positive-loop charges; E-24 executable visiting/resolution; E-33 gate integration; E-40 all exact word choices. All inherited meter, witness and reconsideration obligations remain. SOURCE — The former O1 owner wait is answered only at bound two (0 then1). [decision: owner-record-2026-11-11-loop-o1-a-and-arch-fixes.json; E-22/O1 A, bound two only] PROPOSED — General positive equations and positive-loop setup/turn/counter charges remain open; spelling/profile and other existing technical holds are unchanged.


## EV-122 — Structural events and ordinary/memo call composition

- revision: 2
- standing: DRAFT — transcription of the positively reviewed EV-A3 R1 proposal; independent EV-G2 review required; no adoption.
- kind: requirement
- subject: covered evaluator structural and call events after their required predecessors
- from: SOURCE A3_R1:47-62,66-76,84,91-94; Q0097:12-13,17,21-36; G_A3:23-29,104-108,138; DESIGN_R5:923-933; R9_RULES EV-016/025/043/113 R14 sources: owner-record-2026-11-11-web-oq1-oq3-a.json#OQ1, owner-record-2026-11-11-web-oq1-oq3-a.json#OQ3, DIGEST.json#F014.
- text: PROPOSED Under this DRAFT schedule, variable reads and already-admitted program literals each have (A,R,T,W)=(0,0,0,1). A strict let has the same own invoice after its initializer and before binding or body; the initializer runs even if unused. An ordinary non-memo call pays (0,0,0,1) after all arguments and before the body. Parameter binding, return and the semantic call frame are covered by that proposed tariff. These zero structural cells are proposed here: the inherited call unit did not force them or prohibit another T proposal. A memo lookup pays (0,0,0,1) after arguments. For an eligible completed hit whose result reuse satisfies OQ1, that one lookup unit is the whole call-related charge under proposed E-20 candidate A. On a miss, pay the additional (0,1,0,1) atomically after lookup and before the body: call W1 and prospective memo-entry cell R1. Reserving that cell is distinct from installing or reusing a completed result. Case keeps its inherited dispatch invoice, including branch binding; subject and selected body remain separately evaluated. Pay each step after its evaluated predecessors and before successors, retaining an already committed prefix on a later stop. Full prospective constructor R is charged even on canonical reuse. Known invoices use safe uncapped exact arithmetic and atomic payment; known W is not debited twice. All these additions are proposed composition, not adoption of E-20 A or the cell fee. SOURCE — A memo call reaching its own unfinished key has no completed hit and runs under ordinary fuel; it does not reuse an unfinished result or stop early as unknown solely because that key is unfinished. [decision: owner-record-2026-11-11-web-oq1-oq3-a.json; OQ3 A] The additional reservation above remains a conditional tariff proposal; OQ3 does not adopt lookup, miss, entry-cell or restoration fees. SOURCE — Computing a term once and reusing its result is permitted only when the term makes nothing new, has no effect, has no open choice, consumes nothing use-once, and has a result that may be copied. State-freedom alone is insufficient. [decision: owner-record-2026-11-11-web-oq1-oq3-a.json; OQ1 A with Arch condition]
- case: PROPOSED N10 N1 has total4 and an arithmetic-only omission comparison3. N2 has total4 versus3 when only the let fee is free; at fuel3 the proposed schedule stops at the final literal. W22/W24/W26i/W26ii below are conditional whole-run witnesses under this schedule and the inherited operation premises. SOURCE — When an eligible memo call g(4) reaches its own unfinished key, the inner call continues as an ordinary fueled execution; an implementation returning a hit or immediate key-triggered unknown fails OQ3. INFERENCE — In the explicit fixture g(x)=g(x), repeated re-entry eventually exhausts finite ordinary fuel under the conditional positive-call meter. This case supplies no exact numeric budget or universal divergence claim. [decision: owner-record-2026-11-11-web-oq1-oq3-a.json; OQ3 A]
- excludes: PROPOSED No extra ordinary-call debit on an A-profile hit; no skipping strict initializers, constructor-reuse discount or partial miss payment. No completed result from an incomplete entry. SOURCE — Immediate unknown solely at an in-progress key is the dropped OQ3 B branch. [decision: owner-record-2026-11-11-web-oq1-oq3-a.json; OQ3 A] PROPOSED — Multi-argument keys, interrupted/warmed/resumed/restoration contracts and exact fees remain unselected. No proved bound for covered administrative work follows.
- gaps: E-3/E-13/E-20/E-27 retain adoption/integration and alternative-composition debt; E-34/E-37 restoration; static bound assurance and complete meter remain open.

## EV-124 — Entry, return and partial start-admission boundary

- revision: 2
- standing: DRAFT — transcription of the positively reviewed EV-A3 R1 proposal; independent EV-G2 review required; no adoption.
- kind: requirement
- subject: a fresh run's entry/return charges and proposed separation of start validation from separately fuelled checks
- from: SOURCE A3_R1:80-82,96-109,234,238,249; Q0097:15,52,64; G_A3:81-83,116-128,142; DESIGN_R5:547,923,938,944,976,978; R9_RULES EV-015/038/047/063/064/121
- text: PROPOSED Propose run entry as the first execution event, (A,R,T,W)=(0,0,0,1), covering input binding irrespective of still-unselected multiple-input packing. Returning the run result adds (0,0,0,0). Start admission is proposed outside execution fuel. Its partial list includes input/program-literal interning, case name/count/number checks to every depth, refusal of incoming sequences carrying the computed current scope or reachable sequences lacking contents, Rule S starting-place checks and scope-hash calculation. This list is not exhaustive: preserve all other program/value/graph validity, declaration, alias/head consistency, cycle handling, sequence-free program-literal and memo-mark refusal obligations. Refusal before execution, ordinary results and execution exhaustion remain distinct. The complete admission representation, traversal, canonical start/hash and separate admission-resource/exhaustion protocol are missing; no linear bound or start-size cap is supplied. A D-ADM-5 evaluated-definition check is a separate run with its own fuel and record under the same proposed execution schedule; exhausting it is unknown, not no. Such checks retain the mandatory transitive state-free gate and do not finish preliminary admission's protocol. SOURCE — Incoming mathematical naturals have no selected size cap; they are limited by required paid resources. [decision: owner-record-2026-11-11-oc-03-05-08.json; OC-03 A] PROPOSED — Finite representation/admission/resource/exhaustion contracts remain open; this is no free admission or universal successful-admission guarantee.

- excludes: PROPOSED No complete admission algorithm, universal preliminary-refusal resource guarantee, invented mathematical start-size restriction or capped incoming-natural domain, free evaluated-definition checker, serialized outcome schema or multi-input packing. ## EV-125 — Integer unit and equal-weight invoice

- revision: 1
- standing: DRAFT — transcription of the positively reviewed EV-A3 R1 proposal; independent EV-G2 review required; no adoption.
- kind: requirement
- subject: the proposed semantic fuel unit and sum of valid component counts
- from: SOURCE A3_R1:47,51,113-126,139-146,240-241,248; Q0097:14,16-19,52; Q0093:39; G_A3:110-114,130-134; DESIGN_R5:923-931; R9_RULES EV-017-023
- text: PROPOSED One unit is one integer count in A,R,T or W. Under the proposed equal weights, an invoice is A+R+T+W; execution fuel is a natural with no largest value. Both builds use the same schedule. Each separately priced semantic event has a positive invoice; covered binding, return and administration are not additional priced events. This does not prove a uniform reference-work bound: defined steps, covered work and invoice computation still need bounds. The meaning does not calibrate units to seconds or host bytes; physical measurements remain separate. Alternative fixed positive integer weights would change tariffs and require revised tables/thresholds. Exact safe calculation of every eventual bound remains a separate assurance obligation. The proposed entry-by-entry totality/safe-calculation check has not been performed for the whole table and is not the missing unavailable-bound execution witness.
- case: PROPOSED The inherited obtained-operand mul(7,7) invoice is6+6+0+9=21; entry and two reads give W22 whole-run24. Omitting only operation W gives15 and never supplies a positive-T witness. W24/W26 ranges use the five binary comparison settings in N10; they are sensitivity comparisons, not alternative adopted schedules.
- excludes: PROPOSED No arbitrary real/fractional weights, wraparound, unsafe capped invoice arithmetic or second payment for the same known W. No uniform-work theorem, calibrated host-time model, global bound-availability impossibility proof or claim that static assurance executes EV-021.
- gaps: EV-018 positive-T witness and EV-021 unavailable-bound witness remain NOT DONE. No named primitive/input witness is supplied whose required bound cannot be safely worked out before construction. Q0097 also requires concrete fuel for that requested witness. Complete meter, comparison and physical-calculation proofs remain open.

## EV-129 — Bytes-only hash interface with unselected function and carrier

- revision: 1
- standing: DRAFT — conditional transcription of positively reviewed EV-A2 R1; independent EV-G2 required; no adoption.
- kind: requirement
- subject: an obtained bytes input b under an eventual shared named parameterized hash H
- from: SOURCE A2_R1:28,40-42,49-52,87-108,118-119,144-147,156-157,175-179,189; G0182:17,27,35,53-55,63; G_A2_R0:132,162; DESIGN_R5:912,919; PLAN:99,106-107
- text: PROPOSED The current hash domain is bytes only. For bytes b, the result is H(b) for one named function with fixed parameters used identically in both builds. H, its parameters, a fixed positive digest byte length d, and its result carrier remain unselected. Conditional carrier A returns digest bytes of length d; conditional carrier B returns the digest as an unsigned natural under a stated byte order. Propose (A,R,T,W)=(n+1,d+1,0,n+1) under A, or (n+1,8d,0,n+1) under B, for n=len(b). The natural R is a bound on at most 8d bits, including the zero digest; no actual-value discount is taken. W is one semantic event per input byte plus finish, independent of H, not a bound on every candidate's physical work. Kind classification precedes ordinary size calculations; nonbytes, including rows, use actual-parts Rule E and paid bare none. All known cells/work are reserved atomically after arguments and before work/construction; completed argument effects survive later exhaustion. A2_R1 lists SHA-256, SHA3-256, BLAKE2s/BLAKE2b and BLAKE3 only as recalled candidates, not verified standards or selections. Evidence-file SHA-256 chooses no evaluator hash. Under the proposed scope construction only, bytes carrier A would match its proposed byte form; carrier B needs a stated conversion.
- case: PROPOSED N11 T18-T21 are conditional invoice/shape checks with d=32 for illustration only; H remains abstract and no digest oracle is invented. Empty input has own invoice35 under bytes carrier A or258 under natural carrier B. Hash of a row returns none at Rule E own invoice2, not a digest.
- excludes: PROPOSED No hash overload on other values, canonical nonbytes encoding, selected H/d/parameters/carrier/byte order, tested cryptographic standard, inferred scope-byte authority, H-block tariff or concrete digest implementation.
- gaps: E-1 retains H/parameters/d/carrier/byte order; E-3/E-27 retain alternative H-block W and physical/safe-calculation obligations. E-16/E-38 nonbytes canonicalization/overload remains separate and unperformed.


## EV-130 — Proposed P0 natural framing and value codes

- revision: 3
- standing: DRAFT — proposed P0 byte-format transcription under selected numbered-local/Keep/fixed-order policies; independent EV-G2 required; no adoption of the complete byte profile. SOURCE — [decision: owner-record-2026-11-11-oc-05-09-recs.json; OC-05 B; OC-06 Keep; OC-07 A; clarifies owner-record-2026-11-11-oc-03-05-08.json]
- kind: requirement
- subject: the represented finite P0 value domain, with sequence handles atomic and selected fixed scope-length/bytes/count order; complete P0 agreement proof remains conditional
- from: SOURCE A5_R2:54-82,163,188-190,266-273; G0181:14,29,37-39; G_A5_R0:62-74; R11_RULES EV-038/039; PLAN:102-107 R14 sources: DIGEST.json#sharedrow.
- text: PROPOSED P0 writes N(n), for a natural n, as minimal big-endian payload preceded by its byte length L: the one-byte header L when L<255, otherwise ff followed by N(L), then the L payload bytes. Zero has L=0 and N(0)=00. Refuse leading-zero payloads and escaped lengths below255. A byte string is N(length) then its bytes. Value tags are hexadecimal01 natural followed by N(n);02 bytes followed by its byte string;03 row followed by N(field count) and encoded fields;04 case followed by byte-framed choice name, N(k), N(ordinal) and one encoded payload;05 set followed by N(size) and strictly increasing distinct encoded members;06 sequence name followed by byte-framed scope and N(count). A represented choice keeps its complete name/count identity. A sequence value holds its name, never its mutable contents. The proposal is prefix-free on canonical represented values. SOURCE — OC-07 A fixes scope length, scope bytes, then creation count independently of the current run. [decision: owner-record-2026-11-11-oc-05-09-recs.json; OC-05 B; OC-06 Keep; OC-07 A; clarifies owner-record-2026-11-11-oc-03-05-08.json] PROPOSED — P0 byte-order agreement with EV-039 and canonical byte sorting of set members require the complete profile and proof. Representability, including a differently sized scope, is not imported-profile admission. The finite author checks are not a general decoder/admission or unbounded order proof.
- case: PROPOSED N12 reproduces N(0), N(5), N(1000), the printed sequence/result and color cases, and tests the escaped-length boundary with own finite numbers. color/3 and color/4 differ at the count field; a zero-case choice has no case value. Source historical sample counts are not transferred to this check.
- excludes: PROPOSED No adopted complete canonical byte profile, contents-based sequence encoding, nonbytes hash overload, automatic cross-profile acceptance, complete graph/program decoder or collision/injectivity proof.
- gaps: E-1/4/16/23/24: unnamed H, full faithful codec/admission, imported profile/version/origin and replay alignment. SOURCE — OC-07 A fixed order is answered. [decision: owner-record-2026-11-11-oc-05-09-recs.json; OC-05 B; OC-06 Keep; OC-07 A; clarifies owner-record-2026-11-11-oc-03-05-08.json] PROPOSED — Complete order/canonical-set/member/common-profile agreement proof remains open. R14 INFERENCE — Inline row fields duplicate encoded occurrences despite shared immutable nodes: for r0=Row() and r(k+1)=Row(rk,rk), L0=2 and L(k+1)=3+2Lk, hence Lk=5*2^k-3. At k=30 the candidate needs 5,368,709,117 bytes for 31 distinct nodes; streaming alone does not remove encoded-byte work. PROPOSED — E-16 retains a canonical compact graph transport with references as an unselected candidate requiring resource bounds and faithful identity/order decoding, compared against this inline form before selection. No large value or encoding was allocated. [digest: DIGEST.json; sharedrow]

- text: PROPOSED The represented fresh record concatenates byte-value label loom/ev/record/0, byte-value placeholder build name example-build, start by value, byte-framed scope, policy00 (count zero/empty memo), supplied fuel N(1000), terminal outcome and its value if returned, event count/trail, and final last places. Outcome00 means returned with a value;01 means out of fuel. Preliminary refusal makes no run record. Event01 makes a name with length and initial value;02 writes a name/position with from/to values;03 records a name and last place after a read. Events04/05 are reserved names for restoring write/head rollback only, not supplied restoration semantics. Reads are optional in P0: the logged variant permits intermediate-head reconstruction only for the represented make/write/read fragment; the omitted-read variant keeps explicit final heads and represents only that endpoint. An agreement claim must name its variant. Choice declarations are in the program bytes, not repeated in this fixture; records retain all declarations under selected Keep.
